10199 — dumbbell cross romanian deadlift

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 10199 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A Romanian deadlift where the dumbbells reach across the body, training the hamstrings and glutes.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand holding a dumbbell in each hand with a soft bend in the knees.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 10199.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 10199.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand holding a dumbbell in each hand with a soft bend in the knees. 10199.json#/instructions/0
  2. Push your hips back and lower the weights down your legs. 10199.json#/instructions/1
  3. Reach one dumbbell across toward the opposite foot, keeping your back flat. 10199.json#/instructions/2
  4. Drive your hips forward to stand tall. 10199.json#/instructions/3
  5. Alternate the crossing side each rep. 10199.json#/instructions/4

1022 — band standing rear delt row

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1022 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing rear delt row with a band, pulling the handles toward the chest. It trains the rear delts.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and place the band under your feet. Hold the band handles with your palms facing each other and your arms extended in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1022.json#/instructions/0, 1022.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold the band handles with your palms facing each other and your arms extended in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1022.json#/instructions/1

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and place the band under your feet.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1022.json#/instructions/0

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Resistance tube with handles, Resistance band base family. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1022.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and place the band under your feet. 1022.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the band handles with your palms facing each other and your arms extended in front of you. 1022.json#/instructions/1
  3. Bend your knees slightly and hinge forward at the hips, keeping your back straight. 1022.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pull the band towards your chest, squeezing your shoulder blades together. 1022.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly release the tension and return to the starting position. 1022.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1022.json#/instructions/5

1023 — band straight back stiff leg deadlift

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1023 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A stiff-leg deadlift with a band, hinging at the hips with a straight back. It trains the glutes and hamstrings.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and place the band around your upper legs. Hold the band with both hands in front of your thighs, palms facing your body.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1023.json#/instructions/0, 1023.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold the band with both hands in front of your thighs, palms facing your body.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1023.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and place the band around your upper legs. 1023.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the band with both hands in front of your thighs, palms facing your body. 1023.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keeping your back straight and your knees slightly bent, hinge at the hips and lower the band towards the ground. 1023.json#/instructions/2
  4. Feel the stretch in your hamstrings as you lower the band. 1023.json#/instructions/3
  5. Engage your glutes and hamstrings to lift your body back up to the starting position. 1023.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1023.json#/instructions/5

1027 — assisted single leg press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1027 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A machine press done with one leg at a time, helping you build leg strength and fix imbalances.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on the machine and place one foot on the platform.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1027.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the thighs until the source's arm movement begins; use documented handles or loads when present.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on the machine and place one foot on the platform. 1027.json#/instructions/0
  2. Release the safety and lower the platform until your knee is bent about 90 degrees. 1027.json#/instructions/1
  3. Press through your heel to straighten the leg without locking the knee. 1027.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower back with control. 1027.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your hips on the seat. Finish the reps, then switch legs. 1027.json#/instructions/4

10593 — incline jack plank

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 10593 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

A plank with hands on a raised surface, jumping your feet in and out like a jumping jack. It builds core and gets the heart rate up.

Proposed staging
